# VER-MOK-012 oracle 4: the entropy state at every tick boundary, with and without a sink
#
# commit: bb4a21491eff321cbfd14ba3ea794e34535e3033 (working tree, WO-MOK-012 implementation)
# command: cargo test -p Mokiterions --lib -- --exact --nocapture \
#            simulation::tests::a_record_sink_moves_no_entropy_draw_at_any_tick_boundary
#
# The engine's generator advances by one fixed odd increment per draw, so its state is a draw
# counter and equal states imply equal draw counts. Two runs at one configuration -- one with a
# sink, one without -- are compared at every tick boundary, not only at the end. Boundary 0 is
# after `Simulation::new` and before the initialization events are emitted; boundary 1 is after
# they are; boundary n+1 is after tick n. Boundary 0 is therefore not the seed -- construction
# places resources and agents and those placements draw -- and entropy.txt Part 4 measures how
# many draws construction took and shows boundaries 0 and 1 carry the same count, because
# emitting the initialization events draws nothing. An earlier revision of this header said
# "before initialization", which reads as the seed state and is not what is measured here.
#
# Declared seeds 0, 1, 42, 123 and 777; all three policies; tracing off and on; 150 ticks.
# The lines below are printed from inside the assertion itself, so a retained figure cannot
# differ from the figure that was compared.

    Finished `test` profile [unoptimized + debuginfo] target(s) in 0.04s
     Running unittests src\lib.rs (target\debug\deps\mokiterions-3867f5b7d4d681ad.exe)
